I'll have one glove on, the other half pulled up, and I just... stop. Like I'm stuck. I just stand there.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that you fully understand what's happening during those moments when you're stuck or copying others, or do you feel confused or unaware of why it's happening?\n\nPatient: I don't really know. It just happens.\n\nDoctor: Can you describe any other times when you’ve felt physically stuck, moved without purpose, or copied someone else’s movements? Just... stuck like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced episodes where you felt completely frozen, unable to move at all, or someone had to move you because you couldn’t respond?\n\nPatient: I... no. No one's ever had to move me. I just... I told you, I just stand there sometimes. That's it.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you felt completely shut down, where it was hard to respond to things happening around you or you felt like you were moving in slow motion?\n\nPatient: I guess... yeah. Or I keep rubbing my hands together... until they get sore.
